Softmax and SiLU use a 54   vectorisedfloat32 `exp` (Cody–Waie rducion plus a degree-7 Taylor polynomial, with overflow, 55   underflow and NaN handling). 56 - **Nolibm:**`sqrt`usesthe `fsqrt` instruction (`_builtin_sqrtf`). RoPE cos/sin are precomputed at load 57   into`seq_len×head_size/2` tables. The angleis formed exactly as in the reference, infloat32 58   (`freq=1/pow(10000,hd/hs)`, `val = pos*freq`), and then evaluated with our own double-precision 59   exp and sin/cos and rounded to float. Double is used only for this load-time table, not in the main 60   computation. 61 - **Tokenizer:**thesame algorithm as the reference: dummy prefix, UTF-8 codepoint grouping, byte 62   fallback,andgreedy best-score pair merging with the first index winning ties and a `-1e10` 63   threshold.Lookupsuse a hash tableinstead of bsearch. Pair scores are kept per position and only the 64   twoneighboursarerecomputedaftereach merge. The resultis identical, verified by `tests/difftest.py`. 65 - **Decode/PIECE:**thereference`decode()` logic, including the leading-space strip after BOS and 66   `<0xXX>`bytetokensvia the same `sscanf` pattern. The hexoutput covers he C-string bytes, the same as 67   the reference's returned `char*`. 68 - **Protocol:** see INTERFACE.md. stdout is flushed after every line. GENERATE prints each `T` line as soon 69   as itstokenexists. The stop order after each new token is: token 1 → `stop`, then `count == max_new` 70   → `max`,thenneeding a forward at `pos == seq_len` → `context`. 71  72 ## Limitations / judgement calls 73 - Floating-point results are not bit-identical to the reference. The reduction order differs (vector 74   accumulators), FMA is used (clang's default `-ffp-contract=on`; no fast-math), and exp/sin/cos are our  75   own implementations. The observed max-abs logit difference is ≤ 5e-5 over 1024 positions. All 76   argmaxes agreed in the tests run here, but a near-tie in logits could in principle flip a greedy choice. 77 - The kernel is tuned for and uses NEON on arm64 (Apple M1). It will not build on x86. 78 - Edge inputs the interface does not define are handled defensively but arbitrarily: 79   - Unknown commands reply `ERROR ...`.  80   - Out-of-range ids in FIXED/LOGITS are replaced by 0. An out-of-range PIECE id prints `HEX ` with no bytes. 81   - FIXED/LOGITS sequences longer than `seq_len` are truncated to `seq_len`. 82   - `GENERATE 0 ...` answers `DONE 0 max` without running the model.  83   - A piece whose decoded byte is 0x00 prints as empty (`HEX `), matching C-string semantics.  84 - `check.py` passing is necessary but not sufficient. The final gate uses other inputs I have not seen.╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌ Do you want to create NOTES.md?❯1.
